Title: Le Code noir, ou Recueil des règlemens rendus jusqu'à présent, concernant le gouvernement, l'administration de la justice, la police, la discipline et le commerce des nègres dans les colonies françoises, et les conseils et compagnies établis à ce sujet
Creator: The kings of France (edicts, declarations and ordinances of 1675 to 1762)
Publisher: Paris: chez Prault, imprimeur-libraire (title page and privilege)
Publication Date: 1767 (title page: 'M. DCC. LXVII.')
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1767); the archive.org record's rights field reads 'Domaine public' (Bibliothèque Sainte-Geneviève)
Language: fra
Source: archive.org item BSG_8F403_7INV2830RES, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-15 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (415,532) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1767, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: readable French with the long s read as f; headings and numerals are often garbled. Word count of the host text: 68,904. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: A collection of royal acts of 1675 to 1762, not the edict alone. Table at OCR lines 111-210; privilege (24 May 1759) at 215-317. The Edict of March 1685, the Code noir proper, in 60 articles: heading at 1272, Article I at 1331, Article LX at 2342, date and signatures at 2381-2385, registration at Saint-Domingue (6 May 1687) at 2389-2393. The Edict of March 1724 for Louisiana, in 55 articles, at 11063-12459. The last act, an ordinance of 1762, at about 17100-17735; a list of registrations at 17748-17829.
